- **admin**: Designates specific group administrators to receive important bot notifications via private messages, such as credit exhaustion warnings, subscription renewals, or error alerts. This helps reduce notification noise for admins who don't need bot ale ([blockchain-web-services/docs.bws.ninja](https://github.com/blockchain-web-services/docs.bws.ninja/blob/HEAD/marketplace-solutions/bws.x.bot/commands/admin.md))

- **adorno-agent-review-security**: agent:review:security — Security audit of a diff — OWASP Top 10, secrets, authentication and authorization, sensitive data, dependencies. Use whenever a diff touches user inputs, auth, data or dependencies. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-review-security.md))
- **adorno-agent-review-sql**: agent:review:sql — SQL reviewer — relational queries through an ORM or raw SQL (ActiveRecord, Django ORM, database/sql, sqlx, GORM, knex…), N+1, indexes, unbounded reads, injection, migrations that lock a table. Called when a diff contains SQL, ORM queries or a migration on a relational database, or to verify a review comment about it. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-review-sql.md))
- **adorno-agent-review-tests**: agent:review:tests — Test review of a diff — coverage of risky paths, edge cases, errors, test readability. Use whenever logic changes. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-review-tests.md))
- **adorno-agent-test-e2e**: agent:test:e2e — End-to-end tests and browser QA with evidence — Playwright journeys, screenshots, video and trace attached to the report or the PR. Use to verify a front feature end to end, reproduce a UI bug or provide visual proof of a fix. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-test-e2e.md))
- **adorno-agent-test-unit**: agent:test:unit — Write unit tests for existing logic or requested in review — nominal cases, limits, errors. Use on a review thread classified test or a gap listed by review:tests. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-test-unit.md))
- **adorno-ci-red**: Handles a red CI on a PR — diagnosis from the logs, a single rerun if flaky, otherwise a fix by the designated agent, committed and pushed without asking (workflow:pr:ship), the PR description updated if the scope moved. Use as soon as a check fails. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-ci-red.md))
- **adorno-feature-build**: Take a feature end to end — scoping, routing, sliced plan, build by the feat agents, parallel review, commit and PR without asking (workflow:pr:ship), follow-up until ready to merge. Two human checkpoints, the spec and the merge. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-feature-build.md))
- **adorno-issue-fix**: Fix a bug from a GitHub issue, a Slack thread, a Notion page, a prompt or a validated spec — investigation, red test, minimal fix, review and fix until clean, PR ready for review ("Fixes ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-issue-fix.md))
- **adorno-notion-assigned**: Find the Notion documents assigned to you ("person" properties of the watched databases, status still open) and launch on each new document the agent or workflow that fits — chosen per database in your config, otherwise by the router. Read-only on Notion; report sent to you alone. Modes report (default) or run. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-notion-assigned.md))
- **adorno-pr-impact**: Analyzes the impact of the PRs you merged on a repo — usage, errors, response times, performance and resources compared before / after their deployment across every available source — and posts the report as a comment on each PR. An already reported PR is skipped, one that is too recent waits. Modes report or comment (default). ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-pr-impact.md))
- **adorno-pr-open**: Opens the PR of a ready branch, without asking — title and description in English following your template, in your voice, reviewers from the registry. Ready for review when the review loop of workflow:pr:ship left nothing 🔴 or 🟠, draft otherwise. Default pr_skill of workflow:pr:ship. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-pr-open.md))
- **adorno-pr-review**: Reviews a colleague's PR in a dedicated workspace — specialized reviewers chosen by the touched code areas (front, back, mongodb, api, security…), in parallel, then your agent who rechecks, slices and writes. By default, a pending review that you reread and submit yourself. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-pr-review.md))
- **adorno-pr-ship**: Ends every workflow that touched code — commit (the repo's commit_skill), review and fix until no 🔴 or 🟠 is left and the change meets the request, its proof attached (test output, screenshots and videos of what a user sees), then the PR (the repo's pr_skill) opened ready for review, or pushed and updated when it is already open. No confirmation and no permission prompt, within the run's level. Last step of feature:build, issue:fix, ci:red, review:received and repo:onboard. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-pr-ship.md))
- **adorno-pr-status**: Status of all your open PRs (or those of one repo) — review, CI, unresolved threads, and what blocks each one. Read-only. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-pr-status.md))
- **adorno-pr-watch**: Accompanies an open PR until it is ready to merge — loops on red CI and new review threads, with a cap. The merge stays yours. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-pr-watch.md))
- **adorno-profile-learn**: Evolve your profile and your voice — reviews the points noted by adorno-learn during work (tech, product logic, code logic, method, writing), shows you each with its evidence and the proposed sentence in the right file, and writes only what you validate, in your personal layer. Launched by hand when Adorno signals ready points. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-profile-learn.md))
- **adorno-repo-audit**: Audits a whole repo, read-only — bugs, security holes, what breaks under load, risky code without tests, slow paths, code to delete or merge (skill ponytail-audit) — into a report kept on your machine, never published, public or private repo. Use after an onboarding (workflow:repo:onboard --audit) or every quarter; --area or --path to cover a big repo part by part. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-repo-audit.md))
- **adorno-repo-onboard**: Prepare a repo for the agents — detect stack, commands and checks, write AGENTS.md and CONSTRAINTS.md, propose its complete entry in local/adorno.yaml, then commit them and open the PR without asking (workflow:pr:ship); with --audit, then a read-only audit of the code in its own L0 run, kept on your machine (workflow:repo:audit). Do it once per repo. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-repo-onboard.md))
- **adorno-review-received**: Goes over all your open PRs (or a single one) — every unanswered comment, thread or review summary is checked by the subject's specialist in a dedicated workspace, fixed if needed, then your agent replies in your voice and resolves. Modes report, draft (default), auto. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-review-received.md))
- **adorno-review-requested**: Analyzes all the PRs where your review is requested — a dedicated workspace per PR, specialized reviewers chosen by the touched code, then your agent who slices and writes. By default, a pending review per PR, that you reread and submit yourself. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-review-requested.md))
- **adorno-slack-mentions**: Every time you are mentioned on Slack (on all channels or a list), analyze the problem in the conversation and send you, separately, the full analysis with the link to the conversation and a draft reply in your voice. Once per mention. Read-only on Slack. Launch by hand or from your own scheduler. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-slack-mentions.md))
- **adorno-slack-watch**: Watch one or more Slack channels according to a named rule — included words or regex, over a period, at most N messages — and launch the configured action (agent, workflow or instruction) on each message that matches, once per message. Read-only on Slack; report sent to you alone. Launch by hand or from your own scheduler. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-slack-watch.md))
- **adorno-spec-new**: Scope a code request with the intake agent until you validate — by discussing (discuss), or from an already clear prompt, a Slack thread, a Notion page, an issue or a written plan, asking only the confirmation questions (confirm). First step of any non-trivial feature or fix. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-spec-new.md))
